If A^2 near constant 1, then A coefficients are like a probability distribution whose additive convolution is near uniform. This is equivalent to a highly uniform additive measure. Is that possible with nonnegative discrete measure? The additive energy: ∑ c_k^2 = ∑ a_i^2 a_j^2? If c≈1, sum squares≈2n. Also ∑ a_i^2 = (sum c^2?) Not. By Cauchy, ∑ a_i^4 ≤ M * ∑ a_i^2? If M=1.5, OK.

Maybe use "greedy convolution reduction": start with all ones, identify high coefficient k, reduce a at positions i and k-i? Reducing both? Since a*a coefficient high due to many products. We can lower high products by redistributing to positions where sums are lower. This is akin to optimizing distribution of pair sums. It may converge to near flat coefficients. We can implement with dense n=1024 or 2048, using gradient.

Search strategy design:
1. Choose candidate lengths n_list. Since cost and feasible: [512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 5582 maybe]. Use time allocation.
2. For each n, try several random initializations:
   - Uniform: p=1/n.
   - Random lognormal normalized: exp(gamma), maybe heavy tails.
   - Sparse random: pick m ~ c sqrt(n) positions, weights random/exp normalized; zeros elsewhere.
   - Endpoint/Sidon-ish: choose positions via finite field? Maybe later.
   - If height_sequence_1 available and length matches n or can pad/truncate: normalize.
3. For each init, run iterative optimizer with time limit. Track best score using exact convolution (our FFT, maybe final evaluate). Need avoid each task taking all time; allocate fractions.

B. Sparse/weight optimization:
Given support positions S (indices), optimize weights w (nonnegative) to minimize max convolution. This is a finite-dimensional nonconvex problem with m variables (maybe 100-500) and 2n-1 constraints. We can use gradient descent on weights (simplex or nonnegative) more directly. Support can be fixed or dynamic. If support is Sidon-like, optimization easier. We can also choose support randomly and tune weights.

Weight-only gradient: c = A A^T? For fixed support, c_k = ∑_{i,j in S: pos_i+pos_j=k} w_i w_j. This is polynomial multiplication of sparse vector. We can compute via FFT full length. Gradient for LSE wrt w_i same formula restricted. We can keep density vector a with zeros off support; gradient same. We can then zero out small weights and maybe add new positions.

compute_c_sparse(pos, sums_flat, w, min_len):
   W = np.outer(w, w)
   c = np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=W.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(np.float64)
   # Clip tiny negatives none
   return c

Construct function greedy_support(n, target_m, max_count, seed):
   counts = np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=int32)
   selected = np.empty(n? ) ; s=0
   log? use rng.
   positions = np.arange(n)
   used = np.zeros(n,bool)
   current_max = 0
   for step in range(target_m):
      pool = positions[~used]
      if len(pool)==0: break
      if s==0: x=pool[0] or random? Add 0? But position 0 interacts sums = y, could increase. Random start better. Choose x = rng.choice(pool). But greedy first maybe central? For B_h, edge? We can just choose random or 0. To be systematic, choose candidate with minimal future? For s=0 all same; choose random for diversity.
      else:
         sums = pool[:,None] + selected[:s]  # P×s int32 maybe
         touched = counts[sums]
         max_touched = touched.max(axis=1)
         double_touch = counts[2*pool]
         after_max = np.maximum(max_touched + 1, double_touch + 1)
         final_max = np.maximum(after_max, current_max)
         if max_count is not None:
             feasible = final_max <= max_count
         else:
             feasible = np.ones(P,bool)

         else:
             # no feasible; choose min final_max, then max_touched, sum_score
             idx = np.lexsort((sum_score, max_touched, final_max))[0]
             x=pool[idx]
         # update counts
         if s>0:
             new_sums = x + selected[:s]
             counts[new_sums] += 1
         counts[2*x] += 1
         used[x]=True; selected[s]=x; s+=1
         current_max = max(current_max, np.max(counts)? but current_max can update from touched; use after max selected candidate: current_max = max(current_max, int(after_max[idx]))
   return selected[:s], current_max, counts

Weight optimization function using sparse support and precomputed sums matrix. It should be time-limited (e.g., 5s per support). Since n small and m~100, can do many iter.

Pseudo:
def optimize_weights(pos, n, time_limit, target, best_score, best_w, seed):
    m=pos.size; S=np.sqrt(2*n); S2=2*n
    sums_mat = (pos[:,None] + pos[None,:]).astype(np.int32)  # m×m
    min_len=2*n-1
    # init weights equal or heuristic
    w = np.full(m, S/m)
    logw = np.full(m, np.log(w))
    log_min = np.log(max(1e-12*S/m, 1e-15))
    log_max = np.log(max(10*S, 1.0))
    best_w = w.copy(); best_score = compute_score(w)
    eta = 5.0?  # start
    tau = 0.5
    decay=0.98?
    deadline ...
    last_change=...
    for iter_count...
       c = bincount(sums_mat.ravel(), weights=np.outer(w,w).ravel(), minlength=min_len)
       maxc = c.max(); score = 2*n*maxc/S2
       if score < best_score: update best_w, best_score; maybe print
       if best_score < target and iter_count>some: break? continue to improve.
       if time left small break
       # z
       shifted = (c - maxc)/tau
       # exp clipped
       e = np.exp(shifted)  # shifted <=0, min may underflow ok
       z = e/e.sum()
       # gradient
       zmat = z[sums_mat]
       grad = 2.0 * (zmat @ w)
       centered = grad - np.mean(grad)
       # Normalize centered maybe by its max to set eta scale
       gnorm = np.max(np.abs(centered))
       if gnorm > 0: centered_norm = centered/gnorm
       else break
       # Trial steps: base_eta = maybe 1.0? Because centered in [-1,1].
       trials = [1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.05]
       accepted=False
       # current F
       F_cur = maxc + tau * np.log(z.sum())? z sum=1 so F=maxc + tau*0? Wait shifted by max, sum exp((c-max)/tau)=sum e; F=maxc + tau*log(sum e). If z normalized, F=maxc - tau*log(z.sum())? Actually sum e, F=maxc + tau*np.log(sum_e). We can compute sum_e before normalize.
       for step in trials:
           new_logw = logw - step * centered_norm  # step dimensionless
           new_logw = np.clip(new_logw, log_min, log_max)
           # subtract max for exp
           raw = np.exp(new_logw - new_logw.max())
           sraw = raw.sum()
           if sraw <=0: continue
           w_try = S * raw / sraw
           c_try = bincount(...)
           max_try = c_try.max(); score_try = 2*n*max_try/S2
           # F_try
           e_try = np.exp((c_try - max_try)/tau)
           sum_e_try = e_try.sum()
           F_try = max_try + tau*np.log(sum_e_try)
           if F_try < F_cur - 1e-10 or score_try < best_score:
               logw = new_logw; w = w_try; c=c_try; maxc=max_try; score=score_try; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # Maybe perturb: add random noise to logw
           if rng.random()<0.2: logw += rng.normal(0,0.1,m); clip
       # schedule: reduce tau slowly, maybe if no accepted many times increase? 
       tau = max(0.05, tau*0.995) or based iter
    return best_w,best_score

Support generation with all candidates and vectorization details:
def construct_support(n, target_m, init_T, max_T, seed, pool_factor=0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    counts = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int32)
    selected = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int32)
    used = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    positions = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
    s=0
    T = init_T
    current_max = 0
    for step in range(target_m):
        remaining = positions[~used]
        P = remaining.size
        if P == 0: break
        if s == 0:
            x = int(rng.choice(remaining))
            counts[2*x] += 1
            used[x]=True; selected[s]=x; s+=1; current_max=1
            continue
        selected_cur = selected[:s]
        # To limit memory for large n, use pool subset if pool_factor<1? Implement optional.
        if P > 2000:  # for large n, sample subset
            pool = remaining[rng.choice(P, size=min(P, 1000), replace=False)]
        else:
            pool = remaining
        Pp=pool.size
        sums = pool[:, None] + selected_cur[None, :]  # int32 broadcast may upcast? use .astype(np.int32, copy=False)
        touched = counts[sums]
        max_touched = touched.max(axis=1)
        double_touch = counts[2*pool]
        after_max = np.maximum(max_touched + 1, double_touch + 1)
        final_max = np.maximum(after_max, current_max)
        feasible = final_max <= T
        # secondary scores
        sum_score = np.sum(touched, axis=1).astype(np.int64) + double_touch.astype(np.int64)
        # If feasible, select among feasible; else among all.
        indices = remaining? Actually selected within pool.
        if np.any(feasible):
            cand = np.where(feasible)[0]
            # find min final_max among cand (all same <=T maybe some >? all feasible); choose candidate with min max_touched and sum_score
            # lexsort: primary final_max, secondary max_touched, tertiary sum_score
            sort_idx = np.lexsort((sum_score[cand], max_touched[cand], final_max[cand]))
            # To randomize tie, could take first few. Simpler choose first.
            pick = cand[sort_idx[0]]
            # But to add randomness, after sort, if first several have same primary/secondary, random.
            if sort_idx.size > 1:
                # take window of candidates with same final_max and max_touched as first
                f0 = final_max[pick]; m0=max_touched[pick]
                same = (final_max[cand] == f0) & (max_touched[cand] == m0)
                same_local = same  # relative cand
                if same.sum() > 1:
                    pick = cand[same_local.nonzero()[0][rng.integers(same.sum())]]
        else:
            # no feasible; if T < max_T, perhaps choose best and increment T after update.
            sort_idx = np.lexsort((sum_score, max_touched, after_max))
            pick = sort_idx[0]
            # maybe random tie
            if sort_idx.size > 1:
                a0=after_max[pick]; m0=max_touched[pick]
                same = (after_max == a0) & (max_touched == m0)
                if same.sum() > 1: pick = same.nonzero()[0][rng.integers(same.sum())]
        x = int(pool[pick])
        # update counts
        if s > 0:
            new_sums = x + selected_cur  # int32
            counts[new_sums] += 1
        counts[2*x] += 1
        used[x] = True
        selected[s] = x
        s += 1
        old_prev = current_max
        new_candidate_max = int(after_max[pick])
        current_max = max(current_max, new_candidate_max)
        if old_prev < T:
            # If we used best infeasible and T was too low, increase T to current_max maybe
            if new_candidate_max > T:
                T = min(max_T, new_candidate_max)
        # If we had feasible and T remains.
    pos = selected[:s].copy()
    # compute actual max
    actual_T = int(counts.max()) if s>0 else 0
    return pos, actual_T

Need be careful: current_max should reflect max of counts after all updates. We update counts, but current_max after previous steps. after_max[pick] computed before update from touched. Feasible uses final_max = max(after_max, current_max). Good. After update current_max=max(current_max, after_max[pick]). actual T at end counts.max may include max from other sums; same.

If no feasible, and after_max > T, set T to max(T, after_max)? But then next step threshold. We also increment T maybe. Use T = min(max_T, max(T, new_candidate_max))? If max_T larger. Good.

Function sparse_score(pos, w, n):
   m=len; if m==0 inf

Support optimization function details with sums_mat:
def optimize_sparse_weights(pos, n, time_budget, target_score, initial_score, initial_w=None, seed=0):
    m=pos.size; S2=2.0*n; S=np.sqrt(S2)
    sums_mat = (pos[:,None] + pos[None,:]).astype(np.int32)
    sums_flat = sums_mat.ravel()
    min_len = 2*n-1
    # weights init
    if initial_w is None:
       w = np.full(m, S/m)
    else: w = initial_w.copy().astype(float)
    w = np.clip(w, 0, None); s=w.sum(); if s<=0 inf; w = w/s*S
    logw = np.log(w + 1e-300)
    # To make logw relative not absolute, subtract max? Keep as is.
    best_w = w.copy(); best_score = sparse_score_from_sums(sums_flat, min_len, w, n)
    # heuristic random initial: maybe mix equal and inverse counts? not.
    tau = 0.5
    tau_min = 0.05
    max_step = 10.0
    step = 1.0
    stall = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start_time + time_budget
    # We can allow overrun? Use global deadline passed.
    iter_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline and iter_count < 10000:
       iter_count +=1
       # compute c
       W = np.outer(w,w)
       c = np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=W.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(np.float64)
       maxc = float(c.max()); Ssum = float(w.sum())
       score = (2.0*n*maxc)/(Ssum*Ssum) if Ssum>0 else np.inf
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
           best_score = score; best_w = w.copy()
           # print maybe every improvement
       if best_score < target_score*0.999 and iter_count > 50: # enough
           # could continue but not necessary; maybe break to save time
           break
       if score > 1e6: break
       # F and z
       # If tau very small, exp only peaks.
       shifted = (c - maxc)/tau
       # shifted <=0; exp safe; but if many -inf? no, c finite.
       e = np.exp(shifted)
       sum_e = float(e.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(sum_e) or sum_e <= 0:
           e = np.ones_like(c); sum_e = e.sum()
       z = e/sum_e
       F_cur = maxc + tau*math.log(sum_e)
       zmat = z[sums_mat]
       grad_w = 2.0 * (zmat @ w)
       # numerical ensure finite
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_w)): break
       grad_x = w * grad_w
       # center: subtract weighted mean? Use simple mean.
       centered = grad_x - np.mean(grad_x)
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(centered)))
       if scale == 0 or not np.isfinite(scale):
           tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.5)
           continue
       d = centered/scale
       log_min = np.log(max(1e-15*S/max(1,m), 1e-300))
       log_max = np.log(max(10.0*S, 1.0))
       accepted = False
       # try a few steps descending
       for mult in (step, 0.5*step, 0.2*step, 0.1*step):
           new_logw = logw - mult*d
           new_logw = np.clip(new_logw, log_min, log_max)
           # remove max
           raw = np.exp(new_logw - np.max(new_logw))
           sraw = raw.sum()
           if sraw <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sraw): continue
           w_try = S * raw / sraw
           # compute score/F
           W_try = np.outer(w_try,w_try)
           c_try = np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=W_try.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(np.float64)
           max_try = float(c_try.max())
           score_try = (2.0*n*max_try)/(S*S)  # Ssum S
           # F_try
           shifted_try = (c_try - max_try)/tau
           e_try = np.exp(shifted_try)
           sum_e_try = float(e_try.sum())
           if not np.isfinite(sum_e_try) or sum_e_try <=0:
               F_try = max_try
           else:
               F_try = max_try + tau*math.log(sum_e_try)
           if np.isfinite(F_try) and F_try < F_cur - 1e-10:
               logw = new_logw
               w = w_try
               c = c_try; maxc = max_try; score = score_try
               accepted = True
               # Maybe reduce step? If accepted, keep or increase
               if step < max_step:
                   step = min(max_step, step * 1.1)
               stall = 0
               break
       if not accepted:
           stall += 1
           step = max(0.1, step*0.5)
           if stall > 10:
               #扰动
               noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=m)
               logw = np.clip(logw + noise, log_min, log_max)
               # recompute w
               raw = np.exp(logw - max(logw)); w = S*raw/raw.sum()
               stall = 0
               step = 1.0
           tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.9)
       else:
           # slow tau decay if progress
           if score < best_score + 1e-6:
               tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.995)
   return best_w, best_score

def dense_optimizer(n, time_budget, seed, init='random'):
   S=np.sqrt(2*n); x=...
   a = S*softmax(x)
   c conv via FFT; score=2n max/S^2; track.
   tau=1.0 -> 0.05; step=1.0 (d normalized log gradient). For n=1024, L=2048. Gradient via rfft:
      shifted=(c-cmax)/tau; e=exp; z=e/e.sum(); F=maxc+tau log sum_e
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); Z=np.fft.rfft(z,L); corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Z)*A,L)[:n].real; grad_w=2*corr; grad_x=a*grad_w; centered=grad_x-np.mean(grad_x); scale=max(abs); d=centered/scale; new_x=clip(x - step*d, x_min, x_max); raw=exp(new_x-max); a_try=S*raw/sum.
   Line search similar. x_min=log(1e-15*S/n), x_max=log(10*S). Use n=512 or 1024. Time small.
But if sparse succeeds, dense can be skipped. Include as fallback if time remains and best_score > target.

Dense score using FFT:
def dense_convolution(a, L=None):
   n=a.size; need=2*n-1
   if L is None:
      L=1
      while L<need: L <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:need]
   return c
Potential small negative due round; max unaffected? Negative small not max if positives. Could do c = np.maximum(c,0.0) for score but if all zero? sum>0. Use c.real.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    import time? already top import time
    start = time.monotonic()

Let's detail `construct_support`:
```
def construct_support(n, target_m, init_T, max_T, seed, max_pool=1500):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    counts = np.zeros(2*n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    selected = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int32)
    used = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    positions = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
    s = 0
    T = int(init_T)
    max_T = max(max_T, init_T)
    current_max = 0
    for _ in range(target_m):
        if time.monotonic()? No, inside function no deadline. Could not.
        remaining = positions[~used]
        P = int(remaining.size)
        if P == 0: break
        if s == 0:
            x = int(rng.choice(remaining))
        else:
            selected_cur = selected[:s]
            if P > max_pool:
                pool = remaining[rng.choice(P, size=min(P, max_pool), replace=False)]
            else:
                pool = remaining
            Pp = int(pool.size)
            if Pp == 0: break
            sums = pool[:, None] + selected_cur[None, :]
            # ensure int (it is)
            touched = counts[sums]
            max_touched = touched.max(axis=1)
            double_touch = counts[2 * pool]
            after_max = np.maximum(max_touched + 1, double_touch + 1)
            final_max = np.maximum(after_max, current_max)
            feasible = final_max <= T
            sum_score = np.sum(touched, axis=1).astype(np.int64) + double_touch.astype(np.int64)
            if np.any(feasible):
                cand = np.where(feasible)[0]
                sort_idx = np.lexsort((sum_score[cand], max_touched[cand], final_max[cand]))
                pick = int(cand[sort_idx[0]])
                if sort_idx.size > 1:
                    f0 = int(final_max[cand[sort_idx[0]]])
                    m0 = int(max_touched[cand[sort_idx[0]]])
                    same = (final_max[cand] == f0) & (max_touched[cand] == m0)
                    same_idx = same.nonzero()[0]
                    if same_idx.size > 1:
                        pick = int(cand[same_idx[rng.integers(same_idx.size)]])
            else:
                sort_idx = np.lexsort((sum_score, max_touched, after_max))
                pick = int(sort_idx[0])
                if sort_idx.size > 1:
                    a0 = int(after_max[sort_idx[0]])
                    m0 = int(max_touched[sort_idx[0]])
                    same = (after_max == a0) & (max_touched == m0)
                    same_idx = same.nonzero()[0]
                    if same_idx.size > 1:
                        pick = int(same_idx[rng.integers(same_idx.size)])
            x = int(pool[pick])
        # update
        if s > 0:
            new_sums = x + selected[:s]
            counts[new_sums] += 1
        counts[2 * x] += 1
        used[x] = True
        selected[s] = x
        s += 1
        current_max = max(current_max, int(counts[2*x])? no need, actual counts max? We can update using after_max if not s==0)
```

Now, `equal_score_from_support`:
```
def support_ordered_max_count(pos, n):
   m=pos.size
   if m==0 return 0
   sums = (pos[:,None] + pos[None,:]).ravel()
   cnt = np.bincount(sums, minlength=2*n-1)
   return int(cnt.max())
```
Equal score = 2*n*max_count/m^2.

```
def sparse_score_current(w, sums_flat, min_len, n):
   W = np.outer(w,w)
   c = np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=W.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(float)
   maxc = float(c.max())
   S = float(w.sum())
   if S <= 0: return np.inf
   return (2.0*n*maxc)/(S*S), maxc, c
```
Could use current c. We'll implement inline to avoid repeated.

Pseudo optimize code more concretely:
```
def optimize_sparse_weights(pos,n,time_budget,target_score,seed,initial_w=None):
    m = int(pos.size)
    if m == 0: return None, inf
    S2=2.0*n; S=np.sqrt(S2)
    sums_mat = (pos[:,None] + pos[None,:]).astype(np.int32)
    sums_flat = sums_mat.ravel()
    min_len=2*n-1
    if initial_w is None:
       w = np.full(m, S/m)
    else:
       w = np.asarray(initial_w,float).copy()
       w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
       w = np.clip(w,0.0,500.0)
       if w.sum() <= 0: w = np.full(m, S/m)
       w = w * (S / w.sum())
    best_w = w.copy(); best_score = score...
    logw = np.log(w + 1e-300)
    tau=0.4; tau_min=0.05; step=1.0; max_step=8.0; min_step=0.05
    stall=0; rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+777)
    min_log=math.log(max(1e-15*S/max(1,m),1e-300)); max_log=math.log(500.0)
    start=time.monotonic(); hard=start+time_budget; iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < hard and iter < 20000:
       iter+=1
       W=np.outer(w,w)
       c=np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=W.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(np.float64)
       maxc=float(c.max()); Ssum=float(w.sum())
       if Ssum <= 0: break
       score = (2.0*n*maxc)/(Ssum*Ssum)
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
           best_score=score; best_w=w.copy(); stall=0
       if best_score < target_score and iter > 20:
           # continue for a bit? break if very good
           if best_score < target_score*0.99 or time.monotonic()-start > time_budget*0.5:
               break
       if maxc <= 0: break
       shifted=(c-maxc)/tau
       e=np.exp(shifted)
       sum_e=float(e.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(sum_e) or sum_e <= 0:
           e=np.ones_like(c); sum_e=float(e.size)
       z=e/sum_e
       F_cur = maxc + tau*math.log(sum_e)
       zmat = z[sums_mat]
       grad_w = 2.0 * (zmat @ w)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_w)): break
       grad_x = w * grad_w
       centered = grad_x - np.mean(grad_x)
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(centered)))
       if scale <= 0 or not np.isfinite(scale):
           tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.5); continue
       d = centered / scale
       new_logw = logw - step * d
       new_logw = np.clip(new_logw, min_log, max_log)
       raw = np.exp(new_logw - np.max(new_logw))
       sraw=float(raw.sum())
       if sraw <=0 or not finite:
           step*=0.5; continue
       w_try = S * raw / sraw
       Wt=np.outer(w_try,w_try)
       ct=np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=Wt.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(np.float64)
       maxt=float(ct.max()); St=float(w_try.sum())
       scoret=(2n*maxt)/(St*St)
       shifted_t=(ct-maxt)/tau
       et=np.exp(shifted_t); sum_et=float(et.sum())
       if not finite sum_et: Ft=maxt
       else: Ft=maxt + tau*math.log(sum_et)
       if np.isfinite(Ft) and Ft < F_cur - 1e-10:
           logw=new_logw; w=w_try; c=ct; maxc=maxt; score=scoret
           if step < max_step: step = min(max_step, step*1.2)
           stall=0
           # tau decay slight if improving
           tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.998)
       else:
           step = max(min_step, step*0.5)
           stall += 1
           if stall > 12:
               logw = logw + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=m)
               logw = np.clip(logw, min_log, max_log)
               raw = np.exp(logw - max(logw)); w = S*raw/raw.sum(); stall=0; step=max(0.5, min_step)
           tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.9)
    # final normalize best_w
    best_w = best_w * (S / best_w.sum())
    # compute final score direct
    W=np.outer(best_w,best_w); c=...; score=...
    return best_w, score
```
Need `max(logw)` if logw all? Use `np.max`.

In `Ft` if `sum_et` not finite, but exp shifted_t <=0, sum_e ≤ len c, finite. No issue.

Could reduce print inside optimizer: when score < best_score and best_score changes, maybe too many. We can print only if best_score < 1.45 or new local < previous local -0.01? But caller records. Keep minimal:
```
if score < best_score - 1e-9:
   best_score=score
   if best_score < 1.45: print(...)
```
Not necessary.

But `best_score` local may not reflect global. Fine.

Now, dense optimizer similar but shorter. We may not need but include.

Dense optimizer candidate initializations:

- 1 random normal centered
- 2 random lognormal (x = rng.exponential? then normalize)
- 3 from state? We can pass initial_dense.
Function:
```
def optimize_dense(n, time_budget, seed, initial_a=None):
   S=np.sqrt(2*n)
   rng=...
   if initial_a is not None: a=... normalize; x=log(a+eps)
   else:
      t=seed%3
      if t==0: x=np.zeros(n)
      elif t==1: x=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n); x-=mean
      else: x=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n) + 0.5; x-=mean? maybe lognormal
      x = np.clip(x, -3,3); a = S*exp(x-max)/sum
   best...
   L=next_pow2_len(2*n-1); x_min=...; x_max=log(500)
   tau=0.5 step=1.0...
```
Convolution via FFT: `fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1].real`.
Gradient: z length 2n-1, pad rfft. `A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); Z=np.fft.rfft(z,L); corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Z)*A,L)[:n].real`. Is this correlation orientation correct for dense? We need grad_full_i =2∑_k z_k a_{k-i} (valid). Our previous formula for sparse: corr_i = ∑_j a_j z_{i+j}. In FFT ifft(conj(fft(z)) fft(a)) gave ∑_m z_m a_{m-i} with circular. For i=0..n-1, this equals ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j z_{i+j} if L≥2n-1. Good. Use same.
But here z is weighted over c; grad_w_i=2*corr_i. Good.
Then grad_x=a*grad_w; center; update.

Dense line search one candidate. Accept if F decreases.

Now, time management in main.

Let's write main loop robustly:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except:
        total = 2400.0
    deadline = start + max(1.0, total - 30.0)
    state = {'score': float('inf'), 'n':1, 'pos':None, 'w':None, 'dense':[1.0]}
    # state dense fallback [1.0]
    # parent
    ...
    # Generate supports
    target = 1.5027
    safe_target = 1.48
    candidates = []
    configs = [ (1024,2,15,10), (1024,3,15,12), (2048,2,12,8), (2048,3,12,12), (2048,4,12,8), (4096,3,12,5), (4096,4,10,4) ]
    # But maybe 4096 construction with max_pool 1200 okay.
    for cfg in configs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        n, Tinit, margin, seeds = cfg
        target_m = int(np.ceil(np.sqrt(4.0*n*Tinit / target))) + margin
        # cap by counting:
        max_count_cap = Tinit + 3
        cap = int(np.floor((-1 + math.sqrt(1 + 4* (2*n-1) * max_count_cap))/2)) # solve m(m+1)/2 <= cap*(2n-1)
        if target_m > cap: target_m = cap
        # also no more than n
        target_m = min(target_m, n, 800)
        for si in range(seeds):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            sd = int(seed) + int(n)*1000 + si*97
            pos, C = construct_support(n, target_m, Tinit, Tinit+3, sd, max_pool=1500)
            m = int(pos.size)
            if m < max(10, int(0.6*target_m)): continue? maybe record anyway.
            sums = (pos[:,None]+pos[None,:]).ravel().astype(np.int32)
            cnt = np.bincount(sums, minlength=2*n-1)
            max_ord = int(cnt.max()) if m else 0
            sc = (2.0*n*max_ord)/(m*m)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                w_eq = np.full(m, np.sqrt(2.0*n)/m)
                record_best(state, sc, n, pos, w_eq, tag=f'sparse eq n={n} T={Tinit} C={C}')
                if sc < 1.7 and len(candidates) < 300:
                    candidates.append((sc, n, pos.copy()))
        if state['score'] < 1.4:
            # very good; maybe reduce further seeds? break outer to optimize
            break
    # sort candidates
    candidates.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    # Optimize top candidates
    candidates = candidates[:120]
    # Maybe if no candidates and state.score > target, do dense fallback.
    # Allocate optimization: use while time
    # We'll do a simple loop with budgets that shrink.
    rank = 0
    # first, if state has a sparse best, put it first? candidates may include it.
    # We can create a list of unique supports by (n, tuple(pos)) to avoid duplicates? Not needed.
    for rank, (sc,n,pos) in enumerate(candidates):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 2.0: break
        # budget: top 1 120s? We have lots. Use total 1800s for top candidates.
        # Let's allocate based on rank: bud = 1800/(rank+1) / H? But sum infinite. Simpler:
        if rank == 0: bud = 120.0
        elif rank < 5: bud = 60.0
        elif rank < 15: bud = 20.0
        elif rank < 40: bud = 8.0
        elif rank < 80: bud = 3.0
        else: bud = 1.2
        bud = min(bud, remaining * 0.7 if rank==0 else remaining * 0.9)
        # If score already better than state best by a lot? Still optimize.
        # Stop if current state score < 1.40 and rank>0? maybe still can improve but no need; break to preserve time? We can break if state['score'] < 1.40 to save, but we can use time to improve.
        if state['score'] < 1.40 and rank > 5:
            # still maybe optimize best, break others
            break
        w_best, sc_best = optimize_sparse_weights(pos, n, bud, safe_target, seed=int(seed)+rank*13+1, initial_w=None)
        if w_best is not None:
            record_best(state, sc_best, n, pos, w_best, tag=f'opt rank{rank}')
    # If time remains, refine current best sparse with remaining capped.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60 and state['pos'] is not None and state['w'] is not None:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        bud = min(600.0, remaining * 0.5)
        if bud > 5.0:
            w_best, sc_best = optimize_sparse_weights(state['pos'], state['n'], bud, 1.45, int(seed)+999, initial_w=state['w'])
            record_best(state, sc_best, state['n'], state['pos'], w_best, tag='refine best')
    # If state still above target and time remains, dense fallback
    if state['score'] > 1.5027 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
        for nd in (512, 1024):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
           for sd in range(3):
               bud = min(60.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.25)
               a_best, sc_best = optimize_dense(nd, bud, int(seed)+sd*31)
               if a_best is not None:
                   record_best(state, sc_best, nd, None, None, dense=a_best, tag=f'dense {nd}')
    # Final if still inf fallback
    if state['score'] >= float('inf') or state['dense'] is None:
        state = {'score':2.0,'n':1,'pos':None,'w':None,'dense':[1.0]}
    # exact check
    seq_list = build final
    # If time left > 5 and len seq <= 10000:
    # try exact = evaluate_sequence(seq_list)
    # if finite and exact < state score: print; if exact greater? no action.
    return [float(x) for x in seq_list]
```

Detailed functions:

`next_pow2_len`:
```
def next_pow2_len(need):
    L=1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    return int(L)
```

`softmax_weights_from_log`:
```
def normalize_log_weights(logw, scale):
    logw = np.asarray(logw, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    maxv = np.max(logw)
    if not np.isfinite(maxv):
        raw = np.ones(logw.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        raw = np.exp(logw - maxv)
        s = float(raw.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            raw = np.ones(logw.size, dtype=np.float64); s=float(raw.size)
    return scale * raw / s
```

`sequence_score_fftw`:
```
def sequence_score_fftw(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1: a=a.ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.inf
    L=next_pow2_len(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxc): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*maxc/(S*S))
```
For dense optimizer, we might need c also; separate? Could return c optional? For simplicity, score only. Dense optimizer computes own.

`record_best`:
```
def record_best(state, score, n, pos, w, dense=None, tag=''):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    if score >= state.get('score', float('inf')) - 1e-11: return
    if dense is not None:
        arr=np.asarray(dense,dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if arr.ndim !=1: arr=arr.ravel()
        if arr.size ==0: return
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
        arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
        S=float(arr.sum())
        if S <0.01: return
        state['dense']=arr
        state['pos']=None
        state['w']=None
        state['n']=int(arr.size)
    else:
        if pos is None or w is None: return
        pos=np.asarray(pos,dtype=np.int32).ravel()
        w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if pos.size ==0 or pos.size != w.size: return
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(pos)): return
        n=int(n)
        if n <=0 or n > 200000: return
        w=np.clip(w,0.0,1000.0)
        seq=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
        seq[pos]=w
        S=float(seq.sum())
        if S <0.01: return
        state['dense']=seq
        state['pos']=pos.copy()
        state['w']=w.copy()
        state['n']=n
    state['score']=float(score)
    print(f"NEW BEST {tag}: {state['score']:.8f} (n={state['n']})")
```
If state score initial inf. Good.

But if record with sparse score from equal exact and state already has better, returns. If score equal within 1e-11 no update.

`support_max_order_score(n,pos)`:
```
def support_equal_score(n,pos):
    m=pos.size
    if m==0: return np.inf
    sums=(pos[:,None]+pos[None,:]).ravel().astype(np.int32)
    cnt=np.bincount(sums, minlength=2*n-1)
    max_ord=int(cnt.max())
    return float((2.0*n*max_ord)/(m*m))
```
This computes ordered count, not unordered. Good. For equal weights score with S=sqrt(2n). Yes.

```
def sparse_current(w, sums_flat, min_len, n):
    W = np.outer(w, w)
    c = np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=W.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(np.float64)
    maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    S=sum(w)
    score=(2*n*maxc)/(S*S)
    return c, maxc, score
```

def dense_current(a, L, n):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
    maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0

    return c,maxc,score
```

Now, more detailed code for `optimize_sparse_weights`:
```
def optimize_sparse_weights(pos, n, time_budget, target_score, seed, initial_w=None):
    pos = np.asarray(pos, dtype=np.int32).ravel()
    m = int(pos.size)
    if m <= 0: return None, float('inf')
    n = int(n)
    min_len = 2*n -1
    S2 = 2.0*n
    S = math.sqrt(S2)
    sums_mat = (pos[:,None] + pos[None,:]).astype(np.int32)
    sums_flat = sums_mat.ravel()
    if initial_w is None:
       w = np.full(m, S/m, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
       w = np.asarray(initial_w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if w.size != m: ...
       w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
       w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 500.0)
       sm = float(w.sum())
       if sm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sm): w = np.full(m,S/m); sm=S
       w = w * (S/sm)
    best_w = w.copy()
    c,maxc,best_score = sparse_current(w,sums_flat,min_len,n)
    logw = np.log(w + 1e-300)
    tau=0.5; tau_min=0.05; step=1.0; min_step=0.05; max_step=8.0; stall=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed) & 0xffffffff)
    min_log=math.log(max(1e-15 * S / max(1,m), 1e-300))
    max_log=math.log(500.0)
    start=time.monotonic(); hard=start+max(0.1, float(time_budget))
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < hard and it < 20000:
       it +=1
       c,maxc,score = sparse_current(...)
       if finite score < best_score: best...
       if best_score < target_score and it > 30 and time.monotonic() - start > 2.0:
           # continue maybe not break? break if very good
           if best_score < target_score - 0.02: break
       if maxc <=0: break
       shifted = (c - maxc) / tau
       e = np.exp(shifted)
       sum_e = float(e.sum())
       if not finite or <=0: e=np.ones_like(c); sum_e=float(c.size)
       z = e / sum_e
       F_cur = maxc + tau * math.log(sum_e)
       zmat = z[sums_mat]
       grad_w = 2.0 * (zmat @ w)
       if not finite grad_w: break
       grad_x = w * grad_w
       centered = grad_x - np.mean(grad_x)
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(centered)))
       if scale <=0 or not finite:
           tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.5); continue
       d = centered / scale
       new_logw = logw - step*d
       new_logw = np.clip(new_logw, min_log, max_log)
       w_try = log_weights_to_array(new_logw, S)
       ct,maxt,scoret = sparse_current(w_try, sums_flat, min_len, n)
       shifted_t=(ct-maxt)/tau
       et=np.exp(shifted_t)
       sum_et=float(et.sum())
       if not finite or <=0: Ft=maxt
       else: Ft = maxt + tau*math.log(sum_et)
       if finite Ft and Ft < F_cur - 1e-10:
           logw = new_logw
           w = w_try
           # maybe reduce tau slightly
           if score < best_score + 1e-7:
               tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.997)
           if step < max_step:
               step = min(max_step, step*1.1)
           stall=0
       else:
           step = max(min_step, step*0.5)
           stall +=1
           if stall > 12:
               logw = logw + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=m)
               logw = np.clip(logw, min_log, max_log)
               w = log_weights_to_array(logw, S)
               stall=0
               step=1.0
           tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.9)
    w = best_w; w = w * (S / w.sum())
    c,maxc,score = sparse_current(w,sums_flat,min_len,n)
    return w, score
```

    pos = state.get('pos')
    w = state.get('w')
    n = int(state.get('n', 0))
    if pos is not None and w is not None and n > 0:
        pos = np.asarray(pos, dtype=np.int32).ravel()

Let's produce final. Ensure no analysis outside.```python
"""
Perturb-and-construction search for nonnegative step functions.

The main idea is to exploit the scale invariance of the objective and to look
for sparse supports whose pair-sum multiplicities are small.  For a support
S of size m and common weight sqrt(2n)/m, the score is
2*n*max_k #(i,j in S: i+j=k)/m^2.  Thus low additive-energy sets quickly
beat the trivial constant-function bound 2.  The code:

1. Generates many bounded-pair-sum supports by randomized greedy construction.
2. For promising supports, optimizes the weights with a smoothed-max LSE
   gradient method on the simplex.
3. Keeps a dense FFT-based optimizer as a fallback.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
import operator

# Internal safe target: aim to finish a little below the required bound.
SAFE_TARGET = 1.48
REQUIRED_TARGET = 1.5027


def next_pow2_len(need):
    """Smallest power of two >= need."""
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    return int(L)


def log_weights_to_array(logw, scale):
    """Convert log-domain variables to a nonnegative vector with sum scale."""
    logw = np.asarray(logw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if logw.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxv = float(np.max(logw))
    if not np.isfinite(maxv):
        raw = np.ones(logw.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(raw.size)
    else:
        raw = np.exp(logw - maxv)
        raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
        s = float(raw.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            raw = np.ones(logw.size, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(raw.size)
    return (scale / s) * raw


def sequence_score_fftw(arr):
    """Fast proxy for evaluate_sequence using FFT (scale-invariant)."""
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2_len(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:need]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc / (S * S))


def record_best(state, score, n, pos, w, dense=None, tag=""):
    """Update the best-state dictionary if score is better."""
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    old = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
    if score >= old - 1e-11:
        return

    if dense is not None:
        arr = np.asarray(dense, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = float(arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 0.01:
            return
        state["dense"] = arr
        state["pos"] = None
        state["w"] = None
        state["n"] = int(arr.size)
    else:
        if pos is None or w is None:
            return
        pos = np.asarray(pos, dtype=np.int32).ravel()
        w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if pos.size == 0 or pos.size != w.size:
            return
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 0 or n > 200000:
            return
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
        w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 1000.0)
        pos_ok = np.isfinite(pos) & (pos >= 0) & (pos < n) & (w > 0.0)
        if not np.any(pos_ok):
            return
        pos = pos[pos_ok]
        w = w[pos_ok]
        seq = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        seq[pos] = w
        S = float(seq.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 0.01:
            return
        state["dense"] = seq
        state["pos"] = pos.copy()
        state["w"] = w.copy()
        state["n"] = n

    state["score"] = float(score)
    print(f"NEW BEST {tag}: {state['score']:.8f} (n={state['n']})")


def construct_support(n, target_m, init_T, max_T, seed, max_pool=1500):
    """
    Randomized greedy construction of a set with small unordered pair-sum
    multiplicity.  It tries to keep the maximum unordered count at init_T and
    relaxes only when necessary.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    n = int(n)
    target_m = int(target_m)
    if target_m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32), 0

    counts = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    selected = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int32)
    used = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    positions = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)

    s = 0
    T = int(init_T)
    max_T = int(max(max_T, T))
    current_max = 0

    for _ in range(target_m):
        remaining = positions[~used]
        P = int(remaining.size)
        if P == 0:
            break

        if s == 0:
            x = int(rng.choice(remaining))
            cand_max = 1
        else:
            selected_cur = selected[:s]
            if P > max_pool:
                pool = remaining[rng.choice(P, size=min(P, max_pool), replace=False)]
            else:
                pool = remaining
            Pp = int(pool.size)
            if Pp == 0:
                break

            sums = pool[:, None] + selected_cur[None, :]
            touched = counts[sums]
            max_touched = touched.max(axis=1)
            double_touch = counts[2 * pool]
            after_max = np.maximum(max_touched + 1, double_touch + 1)
            final_max = np.maximum(after_max, current_max)
            feasible = final_max <= T
            sum_score = np.sum(touched, axis=1).astype(np.int64) + double_touch.astype(np.int64)

            if np.any(feasible):
                cand = np.where(feasible)[0]
                sort_idx = np.lexsort((sum_score[cand], max_touched[cand], final_max[cand]))
                pick = int(cand[sort_idx[0]])
                if sort_idx.size > 1:
                    f0 = int(final_max[cand[sort_idx[0]]])
                    m0 = int(max_touched[cand[sort_idx[0]]])
                    same = (final_max[cand] == f0) & (max_touched[cand] == m0)
                    same_idx = same.nonzero()[0]
                    if same_idx.size > 1:
                        pick = int(cand[same_idx[rng.integers(same_idx.size)]])
            else:
                sort_idx = np.lexsort((sum_score, max_touched, after_max))
                pick = int(sort_idx[0])
                if sort_idx.size > 1:
                    a0 = int(after_max[sort_idx[0]])
                    m0 = int(max_touched[sort_idx[0]])
                    same = (after_max == a0) & (max_touched == m0)
                    same_idx = same.nonzero()[0]
                    if same_idx.size > 1:
                        pick = int(same_idx[rng.integers(same_idx.size)])

            x = int(pool[pick])
            cand_max = int(after_max[pick])

        new_sums = x + selected[:s] if s > 0 else np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32)
        if new_sums.size:
            counts[new_sums] += 1
        counts[2 * x] += 1

        used[x] = True
        selected[s] = x
        s += 1
        current_max = max(current_max, cand_max)

        if s - 1 > 0 and cand_max > T:
            T = min(max_T, int(max(T, cand_max)))

    pos = selected[:s].copy()
    actual_T = int(counts.max()) if s > 0 else 0
    return pos, actual_T


def support_equal_score(n, pos):
    """Score of the equal-weight support sequence (positive weights only on pos)."""
    pos = np.asarray(pos, dtype=np.int32).ravel()
    m = int(pos.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0 or n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    sums = (pos[:, None] + pos[None, :]).ravel()
    cnt = np.bincount(sums, minlength=2 * n - 1)
    max_ord = int(cnt.max()) if cnt.size else 0
    return float((2.0 * n * max_ord) / (m * m))


def sparse_current(w, sums_flat, min_len, n):
    """Convolution coefficients and score for a sparse weight vector."""
    W = np.outer(w, w)
    c = np.bincount(sums_flat, weights=W.ravel(), minlength=min_len).astype(np.float64)
    maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    S = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return c, maxc, float("inf")
    score = float((2.0 * n * maxc) / (S * S))
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return c, maxc, float("inf")
    return c, maxc, score


def optimize_sparse_weights(pos, n, time_budget, target_score, seed, initial_w=None):
    """
    Optimize positive weights on a fixed support using a log-domain
    smoothed-max gradient method with sum normalization.
    """
    pos = np.asarray(pos, dtype=np.int32).ravel()
    m = int(pos.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    S2 = 2.0 * float(n)
    S = math.sqrt(S2)
    min_len = 2 * n - 1
    sums_mat = (pos[:, None] + pos[None, :]).astype(np.int32)
    sums_flat = sums_mat.ravel()

    if initial_w is None:
        w = np.full(m, S / m, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = np.asarray(initial_w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if w.size != m:
            w = np.full(m, S / m, dtype=np.float64)
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
        w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 500.0)
        sm = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sm) or sm <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(m, S / m, dtype=np.float64)
            sm = S
        else:
            w = w * (S / sm)

    best_w = w.copy()
    c, maxc, best_score = sparse_current(w, sums_flat, min_len, n)

    logw = np.log(w + 1e-300)
    min_log = math.log(max(1e-15 * S / max(1, m), 1e-300))
    max_log = math.log(500.0)

    tau = 0.5
    tau_min = 0.05
    step = 1.0
    min_step = 0.05
    max_step = 8.0
    stall = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) & 0x7fffffff)

    start = time.monotonic()
    hard = start + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < hard and it < 20000:
        it += 1
        c, maxc, score = sparse_current(w, sums_flat, min_len, n)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_w = w.copy()
            if best_score < SAFE_TARGET - 0.02 and time.monotonic() - start > 2.0:
                break
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        shifted = (c - maxc) / tau
        e = np.exp(shifted)
        sum_e = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sum_e) or sum_e <= 0.0:
            e = np.ones_like(c)
            sum_e = float(c.size)
        z = e / sum_e
        F_cur = float(maxc + tau * math.log(sum_e))

        z_mat = z[sums_mat]
        grad_w = 2.0 * (z_mat @ w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_w)):
            break
        grad_x = w * grad_w
        centered = grad_x - np.mean(grad_x)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(centered)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
            tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.5)
            continue

        d = centered / scale
        new_logw = logw - step * d
        new_logw = np.clip(new_logw, min_log, max_log)
        w_try = log_weights_to_array(new_logw, S)

        c_try, max_try, score_try = sparse_current(w_try, sums_flat, min_len, n)
        shifted_try = (c_try - max_try) / tau
        e_try = np.exp(shifted_try)
        sum_e_try = float(e_try.sum())
        if np.isfinite(sum_e_try) and sum_e_try > 0.0:
            F_try = float(max_try + tau * math.log(sum_e_try))
        else:
            F_try = float(max_try)

        if np.isfinite(F_try) and F_try < F_cur - 1e-10:
            logw = new_logw
            w = w_try
            if np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < best_score + 1e-8:
                tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.997)
            if step < max_step:
                step = min(max_step, step * 1.1)
            stall = 0
        else:
            step = max(min_step, step * 0.5)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 12:
                logw = logw + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=m)
                logw = np.clip(logw, min_log, max_log)
                w = log_weights_to_array(logw, S)
                stall = 0
                step = 1.0
            tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.9)

    best_w = best_w.copy()
    sm = float(best_w.sum())
    if np.isfinite(sm) and sm > 0.0:
        best_w = best_w * (S / sm)
    _, _, final_score = sparse_current(best_w, sums_flat, min_len, n)
    return best_w, final_score


def dense_current(a, L, n):
    """FFT convolution and score for a dense vector already scaled."""
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return c, maxc, float("inf")
    return c, maxc, float((2.0 * n * maxc) / (S * S))


def optimize_dense(n, time_budget, seed, initial_a=None):
    """Dense fallback optimizer in log-domain for small n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    L = next_pow2_len(2 * n - 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    if initial_a is not None:
        a = np.asarray(initial_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            a = None
        if a is None:
            a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 500.0)
        S0 = float(a.sum())
        if S0 <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S0):
            a = np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
            S0 = S
        else:
            a = a * (S / S0)
    else:
        mode = int(seed) % 3
        if mode == 0:
            x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        elif mode == 1:
            x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            x = x - np.mean(x)
        else:
            x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            x = x - np.mean(x) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, 1)[0]
        x = np.clip(x, -3.0, 3.0)
        a = log_weights_to_array(x, S)

    best_a = a.copy()
    _, _, best_score = dense_current(best_a, L, n)

    min_log = math.log(max(1e-15 * S / max(1, n), 1e-300))
    max_log = math.log(500.0)
    x = np.log(a + 1e-300)

    tau = 0.8
    tau_min = 0.05
    step = 1.0
    min_step = 0.05
    max_step = 6.0
    stall = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    hard = start + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < hard and it < 20000:
        it += 1
        c, maxc, score = dense_current(a, L, n)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            if best_score < SAFE_TARGET - 0.02 and time.monotonic() - start > 2.0:
                break
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        shifted = (c - maxc) / tau
        e = np.exp(shifted)
        sum_e = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sum_e) or sum_e <= 0.0:
            e = np.ones_like(c)
            sum_e = float(c.size)
        z = e / sum_e
        F_cur = float(maxc + tau * math.log(sum_e))

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        Z = np.fft.rfft(z, L)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Z) * A, L)[:n]
        corr = np.where(np.isfinite(corr), corr, 0.0)
        grad_w = 2.0 * corr
        grad_x = a * grad_w
        centered = grad_x - np.mean(grad_x)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(centered)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
            tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.5)
            continue

        d = centered / scale
        new_x = np.clip(x - step * d, min_log, max_log)
        a_try = log_weights_to_array(new_x, S)

        c_try, max_try, score_try = dense_current(a_try, L, n)
        shifted_try = (c_try - max_try) / tau
        e_try = np.exp(shifted_try)
        sum_e_try = float(e_try.sum())
        if np.isfinite(sum_e_try) and sum_e_try > 0.0:
            F_try = float(max_try + tau * math.log(sum_e_try))
        else:
            F_try = float(max_try)

        if np.isfinite(F_try) and F_try < F_cur - 1e-10:
            x = new_x
            a = a_try
            if np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < best_score + 1e-8:
                tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.997)
            if step < max_step:
                step = min(max_step, step * 1.1)
            stall = 0
        else:
            step = max(min_step, step * 0.5)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 12:
                x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n), min_log, max_log)
                a = log_weights_to_array(x, S)
                stall = 0
                step = 1.0
            tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.9)

    sm = float(best_a.sum())
    if np.isfinite(sm) and sm > 0.0:
        best_a = best_a * (S / sm)
    _, _, final_score = dense_current(best_a, L, n)
    return best_a, final_score


def finalize_sequence(state):
    """Build a clean numpy array from the best state."""
    pos = state.get("pos")
    w = state.get("w")
    n = int(state.get("n", 0))
    if pos is not None and w is not None and n > 0:
        pos = np.asarray(pos, dtype=np.int32).ravel()
        w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if pos.size == w.size and pos.size > 0:
            ok = np.isfinite(pos) & (pos >= 0) & (pos < n) & np.isfinite(w) & (w >= 0.0)
            if np.any(ok):
                seq = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                seq[pos[ok]] = w[ok]
                seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if float(seq.sum()) >= 0.01:
                    return seq

    dense = state.get("dense")
    if dense is not None:
        arr = np.asarray(dense, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0:
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(arr.sum()) >= 0.01:
                return arr

    return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for the best nonnegative step sequence within the time budget."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 2400.0
    total = max(1.0, total)
    reserve = max(5.0, min(60.0, total * 0.02))
    deadline = start + max(0.5, total - reserve)

    state = {
        "score": 2.0,
        "n": 1,
        "pos": None,
        "w": None,
        "dense": np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64),
    }

    # Use supplied parent if available and affordable.
    try:
        parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if parent is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            n0 = int(arr.size)
            if 0 < n0 <= 20000:
                arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                S0 = float(arr.sum())
                if S0 > 0.01:
                    arr = arr * (math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n0)) / S0)
                    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                    sc = sequence_score_fftw(arr)
                    record_best(state, sc, n0, None, None, dense=arr, tag="parent")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("parent handling skipped:", exc)

    candidates = []

    # Greedy support constructions.
    configs = [
        (1024, 2, 8, 12),
        (1024, 3, 8, 14),
        (2048, 2, 10, 10),
        (2048, 3, 10, 12),
        (2048, 4, 10, 8),
        (4096, 2, 12, 5),
        (4096, 3, 12, 7),
    ]

    for n, Tinit, margin, seeds in configs:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        target_m = int(np.ceil(np.sqrt(4.0 * float(n) * float(Tinit) / REQUIRED_TARGET))) + int(margin)
        max_count_cap = int(Tinit + 3)
        cap = int(np.floor((-1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * float(2 * n - 1) * float(max_count_cap))) / 2.0))
        target_m = max(2, min(target_m, cap, int(n), 800))

        for si in range(int(seeds)):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            sd = int(seed) + n * 997 + si * 131
            pos, C = construct_support(n, target_m, Tinit, Tinit + 3, sd, max_pool=1500)
            m = int(pos.size)
            if m < 8:
                continue
            sc = support_equal_score(n, pos)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                w_eq = np.full(m, math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)) / m, dtype=np.float64)
                record_best(state, sc, n, pos, w_eq, tag=f"eq n={n} Tinit={Tinit} C={C} m={m}")
                if sc < 1.8 and len(candidates) < 350:
                    candidates.append((sc, n, pos.copy()))

        if state["score"] < SAFE_TARGET - 0.05:
            # The current best is already comfortably below the required bound.
            # Keep a few seconds of runtime, then stop construction early.
            if time.monotonic() - start > 45.0:
                break

    if state["score"] < SAFE_TARGET - 0.05 and time.monotonic() - start > 60.0:
        return [float(x) for x in finalize_sequence(state)]

    # A few plain random supports can occasionally help.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline and state["score"] > SAFE_TARGET:
        rng0 = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 12345)
        for nr in (1024, 2048):
            m_rand = int(np.ceil(np.sqrt(8.0 * float(nr) / REQUIRED_TARGET))) + 12
            for _ in range(10):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                pos = rng0.choice(nr, size=min(nr, m_rand), replace=False).astype(np.int32)
                sc = support_equal_score(nr, pos)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    w_eq = np.full(pos.size, math.sqrt(2.0 * float(nr)) / pos.size, dtype=np.float64)
                    record_best(state, sc, nr, pos, w_eq, tag=f"rand n={nr} m={pos.size}")
                    if sc < 1.8 and len(candidates) < 350:
                        candidates.append((sc, nr, pos.copy()))

    candidates.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    candidates = candidates[:150]

    # Weight optimization for the most promising supports.
    for rank, item in enumerate(candidates):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 2.0:
            break
        sc0, n, pos = item
        if state["score"] < SAFE_TARGET - 0.05 and rank > 8:
            break

        if rank == 0:
            bud = 120.0
        elif rank < 5:
            bud = 45.0
        elif rank < 15:
            bud = 15.0
        elif rank < 40:
            bud = 5.0
        elif rank < 90:
            bud = 2.0
        else:
            bud = 1.0
        bud = min(bud, remaining * 0.9)

        w_best, sc_best = optimize_sparse_weights(
            pos, n, bud, SAFE_TARGET, int(seed) + rank * 17 + 1, initial_w=None
        )
        if w_best is not None and np.isfinite(sc_best):
            record_best(state, sc_best, n, pos, w_best, tag=f"opt rank={rank}")

    # Refine the current best sparse solution if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        if state.get("pos") is not None and state.get("w") is not None:
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            bud = min(600.0, remaining * 0.6)
            if bud > 5.0:
                w_best, sc_best = optimize_sparse_weights(
                    state["pos"],
                    state["n"],
                    bud,
                    SAFE_TARGET - 0.03,
                    int(seed) + 99999,
                    initial_w=state["w"],
                )
                if w_best is not None and np.isfinite(sc_best):
                    record_best(state, sc_best, state["n"], state["pos"], w_best, tag="refine best")
        else:
            # Dense fallback if sparse representation is unavailable/weak.
            if state["score"] > REQUIRED_TARGET:
                for nd in (512, 1024):
                    for sd in range(3):
                        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10.0:
                            break
                        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                        bud = min(60.0, remaining * 0.35)
                        a_best, sc_best = optimize_dense(nd, bud, int(seed) + sd * 29)
                        if a_best is not None and np.isfinite(sc_best):
                            record_best(state, sc_best, nd, None, None, dense=a_best, tag=f"dense n={nd} seed={sd}")

    seq = finalize_sequence(state)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in seq]

    # Optional authoritative check near the end.
    try:
        if 0 < len(final_list) <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))  # assumed to be imported
            print(f"FINAL EXACT SCORE: {exact:.8f}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("final exact check skipped:", exc)

    return final_list
```
